# Skills · A · part 273

840 entries, ASR to assess. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ASR**: Implement speech-to-text (ASR/automatic speech recognition) capabilities using any OpenAI-compatible server that exposes the /audio/transcriptions endpoint. Use this skill when the user needs to transcribe audio files, convert speech to text, build voice input features, or process audio recordings. Supports multipart audio uploads and returns accurate text transcriptions. Works with any OpenAI-compatible LLM server (open-source or free), designed for the DiveeOI agent (OpenCode-based). ([abdou-da0wew/zcode-skills](https://github.com/abdou-da0wew/zcode-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ASR))
- **asr**: &gt; 更新时间：2026-04-12 &gt; 来源：https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/speech-recognition ([brantzh6/AI-skills](https://github.com/brantzh6/AI-skills/tree/HEAD/aliyun-bailian/knowledge/asr))
- **asr**: Insert **DropCowboy ASR** when the user needs transcription of call recordings or uploaded media already in DropCowboy. ([Drop-Cowboy/dropcowboy](https://github.com/Drop-Cowboy/dropcowboy/tree/HEAD/skills/asr))
- **asr**: Fast, accurate, and incredibly inexpensive automatic speech-to-text. Use when the user needs asr functionality. ([KingKaonix/minis-skills](https://github.com/KingKaonix/minis-skills/tree/HEAD/public/skills/asr) · 2 copies)
- **ASR**: Transcribe speech to text from audio files (and audio tracks of video) — meetings, voice notes, podcasts, interviews. Use when the user wants an audio file turned into text, a transcript, captions, or to extract what was said. ([Kirazul/Vaultgate-Agent](https://github.com/Kirazul/Vaultgate-Agent/tree/HEAD/src/skills/ASR))
- **asr**: 녹음을 화자별로 나눈 한국어 전사본으로 만드는 스킬. 역할은 회의·면접·통화·인터뷰 녹음(m4a·wav·mp3, 음성 메모)을 받아 누가 언제 무슨 말을 했는지 정확히 받아쓰는 것까지다. 기능은 화자 분리와 타임스탬프, 목소리가 같은 화자 합치기, 음량이 매우 낮은 녹음 자동 보정(원본은 그대로), 인식에 실패한 구간 표시, 전사 뒤 화자 이름 바꾸기("화자1은 김철수")다. 결과는 전사본(.md)과 데이터(.json)다. 녹음을 받아쓰거나 누가 뭐라고 했는지 정리해 달라는 요청, 음성 파일을 건네는 경우에 쓴다. Apple Silicon Mac에서 로컬로만 처리한다. 요약, 회의록 정리, 면접 평가는 하지 않고 다른 스킬에 넘긴다. ([nyjin/skills](https://github.com/nyjin/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/asr))
- **ASR**: Speech-to-text (Automatic Speech Recognition) guide. Use when: transcribing audio, converting speech to text, building voice input features, processing audio recordings, meeting transcription, podcast transcription, or any audio-to-text task. ([Prathviraj-jadhav/nexgen-elit-website](https://github.com/Prathviraj-jadhav/nexgen-elit-website/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/ASR))

- **ASR Transcribe to Text (Qwen3-ASR)**: Use when the user wants to transcribe audio/video, do speech-to-text, or convert meeting recordings/lectures/interviews/podcasts to text — uses Qwen3-ASR via local MLX on Apple Silicon (no API key) or a remote vLLM/OpenAI-compatible endpoint. ([aashutosh396/mindpalace](https://github.com/aashutosh396/mindpalace/tree/HEAD/mindpalace/skills/global/media/asr-transcribe-to-text))

- **asr-archive-to-edited-docs**: Turn existing diarized ASR transcripts into raw speaker-split records, readable edited transcripts and detailed summaries. Use for cleaning lecture, seminar, interview or meeting transcripts, including archives with duplicates, split sessions and gaps. Recognition of audio itself is outside this skill. ([IgorOberon/translate-pdf-latex](https://github.com/IgorOberon/translate-pdf-latex/tree/HEAD/companion-skills/asr-archive-to-edited-docs) · ★16)
- **asr-bambara-eval**: Evaluates automatic speech recognition (ASR) models on spontaneous speech in Bambara, a low-resource West African language. It probes the models' ability to accurately transcribe audio segments in both a controlled test set and a more heterogeneous benchmark. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Afvoices Test, Nyana Eval, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports WER (%). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/asr-bambara-eval) · ★3)
- **asr-benchmark-dataset-curation**: Curate ASR benchmark datasets so that speech-model evaluations rest on documented, consented, and versioned corpora. ([SamoTech/skills-tree](https://github.com/SamoTech/skills-tree/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/asr-benchmark-dataset-curation) · ★4)
- **asr-claw**: Speech recognition CLI for AI agent automation. Transcribe audio from stdin, files, or URLs. ([llm-net/asr-claw](https://github.com/llm-net/asr-claw/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-claw))
- **asr-cleaner**: 已转写录音文字的统一处理入口：接收用户提供的原始 ASR 文本或文本文件，做可追溯的忠实清洗，并通过显式 handoff 自动调用 asr-life-log 分类归档到 Obsidian。用户说「清洗 ASR」「整理这段转写并归档」「帮我清洗这段录音文字」时使用。只接受文字，不接收或转写音频/视频；若文本已清洗或已语义压缩且只需归档，可直接使用 asr-life-log。 ([CamellionKid/asr-wiki-pipeline](https://github.com/CamellionKid/asr-wiki-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-cleaner))
- **asr-clinical-continual-eval**: This evaluation probes an ASR model's ability to continuously adapt to noisy, rural clinical telephony speech while retaining its baseline performance on standard general-domain speech. It specifically measures the trade-off between target-domain transcription accuracy and catastrophic forgetting of pre-trained linguistic knowledge. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Gram Vaani, Kathbath, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports WER. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/asr-clinical-continual-eval) · ★3)
- **asr-configurator**: Pick an ASR model (Whisper variant / Moonshine / faster-whisper) and decoding parameters for a new speech pipeline. Use when you need help with asr configurator. ([anubhavg-icpl/vibe](https://github.com/anubhavg-icpl/vibe/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-configurator) · ★13)
- **asr-coprocessor-eval**: Evaluates the training efficiency and final accuracy of end-to-end acoustic models for Automatic Speech Recognition across different CPU-GPU co-processor hardware configurations. It measures how quickly models reach specific accuracy targets (Time-to-Accuracy) and compares final error rates against baseline architectures. Use when the user wants to benchmark on ASR_Dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports TTA. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/asr-coprocessor-eval) · ★3)
- **asr-data-analysis**: Use when executing and reporting the analysis for an American Sociological Review (ASR) manuscript so it survives expert masked review — honest uncertainty, robustness, and evidence handling appropriate to quantitative, demographic, comparative-historical, or computational sociology. Guides analysis norms; it does not fabricate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/American-Sociological-Review-Skills/skills/asr-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 4 copies)
- **asr-data-and-transparency**: Use when handling data documentation, sharing, and confidentiality for an American Sociological Review (ASR) manuscript. ASR follows the ASA data-sharing policy — share data and documentation after a project's completion or major publications, with confidentiality and proprietary exemptions — rather than a mandatory editor-verified replication deposit. Prepares documentation; it does not over-state requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/American-Sociological-Review-Skills/skills/asr-data-and-transparency) · ★1.4k · 4 copies)
- **asr-datasets-benchmarks-eval**: Evaluates Automatic Speech Recognition systems across diverse acoustic conditions, domains, and linguistic settings. It probes model robustness to read vs. spontaneous speech, clean vs. noisy environments, and demographic bias in multilingual crowdsourced data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on LibriSpeech, Switchboard, TED-LIUM 3, CHiME-6, Common Voice 17.0, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Word Error Rate (WER). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/asr-datasets-benchmarks-eval) · ★3)
- **asr-discovery**: Identify evidence-backed Architecture Significant Requirement candidates from requirements, NFRs, constraints, risks, architecture impact, and an Architecture Context Pack. Use after architecture context and impact are understood, when an architect must distinguish ordinary requirements from concerns that may materially shape the architecture. Do not approve ASRs, invent quality targets, choose technologies, design the target architecture, or create ADRs. ([ianache/training-manager](https://github.com/ianache/training-manager/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/asr-discovery))
- **asr-eval**: Measure recognition quality in Russian and English — WER, CER, hallucinations on silence, coverage of the audio sent, speed, and memory. Use when changing the ASR model or engine, when tuning VAD or chunking, and when transcripts come out poor. Do not use to diagnose the recording itself — that is audio-doctor. ([yhn112/Talkloom](https://github.com/yhn112/Talkloom/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/asr-eval))
- **asr-extractor**: Internal Moonshot Architecture stage for extracting architecturally significant requirements and quality attribute scenarios from normalized requirements. ([munlucky/moonshot-relay](https://github.com/munlucky/moonshot-relay/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-extractor))
- **asr-hallucination-detection**: Detect hallucinated transcription segments in ASR output by flagging text produced over non-speech audio, repeated loop patterns, and empty-audio text before downstream use. ([SamoTech/skills-tree](https://github.com/SamoTech/skills-tree/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/asr-hallucination-detection) · ★4)
- **asr-learn**: Knowledge building skill for ASR error analysis. Analyzes successful CRIs to extract patterns, service behaviors, and timing baselines. Enriches both the vector knowledge base AND kusto-reference.yaml depending on the type of insight. Does NOT log to CRI history (that's for failed CRIs only). ([vigaur/Azure-SiteRecovery-DevTools](https://github.com/vigaur/Azure-SiteRecovery-DevTools/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/asr-learn))
- **asr-lexicon**: 查询和预览语音识别热词表，并执行用户明确给出的完整词表导入，用于录音、视频和实时语音的专有名词识别。 ([qingmingyiyang/Incremind](https://github.com/qingmingyiyang/Incremind/tree/HEAD/config/application-skills/asr-lexicon) · ★1)
- **asr-lexicon-maintenance**: 在工作台查询、添加、修改、调整权重、停用和恢复个人热词，展示变更预览后等待用户确认保存。 ([qingmingyiyang/Incremind](https://github.com/qingmingyiyang/Incremind/tree/HEAD/config/application-skills/asr-lexicon-maintenance) · ★1)
- **asr-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning an American Sociological Review (ASR) manuscript against the literature so it reads as a broad sociological contribution rather than a subfield note. Stakes the contribution in a debate that travels across sociology; it does not write the literature review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/American-Sociological-Review-Skills/skills/asr-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **asr-metric-approx-eval**: Evaluates a label-free regression framework that approximates automatic speech recognition error rates (WER and CER) using multimodal embeddings and predicted transcripts. Probes the tool's robustness across diverse acoustic conditions, domains, and out-of-distribution settings. Use when the user wants to benchmark on LibriSpeech, TED-LIUM, GigaSpeech, SPGISpeech, Common Voice, Earnings22, AMI (IHM), People’s Speech, SLUE-VoXCeleb, Primock57, VoxPopuli Accented, ATCOsim, BERSt, CHiME-6, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports MAE. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/asr-metric-approx-eval) · ★3)
- **asr-model-release-comparison**: Compare newly released ASR models on declared criteria with recorded per-criterion evidence and a dated verdict. ([SamoTech/skills-tree](https://github.com/SamoTech/skills-tree/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/asr-model-release-comparison) · ★4)
- **asr-noise-robustness-eval**: Evaluates the robustness of end-to-end automatic speech recognition models to real-world acoustic distortions, including far-field reverberation, mixed sampling rates, low-bitrate codecs, and background noise at varying signal-to-noise ratios. Use when the user wants to benchmark on LibriSpeech, BUT ReverbDB, Hub5 Switchboard & CallHome, AISHELL-2, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports greedy WER (%). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/asr-noise-robustness-eval) · ★3)
- **asr-notes**: 把录音、口述音频或现成字幕转成可编辑的结构化 Markdown：DeepSeek ASR 转写（自动切片、 逐段打时间戳）+ 两段式清洗与结构化，直接写进 Obsidian 库。也支持 .srt/.vtt/.ass 字幕文件直接进管线（不重复转写、不花转写费）。附可执行脚本、提示词模板和成本护栏。 当用户说"把这段录音转成文字""录音整理成文稿""语音转笔记""跑一下 ASR""这段口述帮我整理" "m4a 转 markdown""会议录音转纪要""把刚才说的话整理一下""录音文件在这里" "字幕转文稿""srt 转文字""把这个字幕文件整理一下""vtt/ass 转 markdown"时使用。 不适用：边说边上屏的实时输入（用输入法自带语音）、纯文本排版与中英文空格（用 cn-typography）、 自己制作字幕文件（打轴/校对时间）、音视频剪辑、把文字转成语音。 ([visionjam/ASR-TTS](https://github.com/visionjam/ASR-TTS/tree/HEAD/agent-skill/asr-notes))
- **asr-patch-casing**: Capitalize sentence starts and proper nouns in an ASR transcript segment. Mirrors capitalization of proper nouns/entities/acronyms from ENGLISH_TRANSLATION onto the corresponding ORIGINAL tokens (for scripts with case distinction). Standardizes casing for no_touch_tokens. Uses seg_start to decide segment-initial capitalization. Strictly letter-case only — no punctuation changes. Use as the CASING step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-casing))
- **asr-patch-combine**: Merge separated fragments of a single entity in an ASR transcript (e.g., "10 0" -&gt; "100", spaced-out IDs, products, names, currencies, dates, times, handles, multipliers) where the whitespace is purely a formatting artifact. Does NOT merge letter-by-letter spellings, does NOT convert numerals or expand years (defer to NUMERAL). Use as the COMBINE step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-combine))
- **asr-patch-disfluency**: Remove fillers, false starts, and noise/non-speech tags (e.g., [NOISE], _bg noise_, [SPN/], _hmm_, [unin/]) from an ASR transcript segment. Preserves long repeated spans (prevents audio-drift) and only collapses short adjacent filler-like repetitions. Never removes content repeated across different speakers/turns. Scales with aggressiveness_level. Use as the DISFLUENCY step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-disfluency))
- **asr-patch-format**: Fix spacing artifacts in symbols, abbreviations, and compact forms in an ASR transcript segment. Removes unnecessary quotation marks (including locale-specific) without changing meaning, grammar, wording, language, script, or order. Preserves compact forms for fixed compounds and technical brands. Treats legal names as protected (format-level only). Use as the FORMAT step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-format))
- **asr-patch-lexical**: Fix typos and phonetic ASR errors in a transcript segment while strictly preserving meaning, pronunciation similarity, token order, and original semantic role. Never reorders tokens to match English syntax. Never replaces a valid token with a different valid token unless ORIGINAL evidence forces the change. Supports standard and conservative modes (conservative forbids register upgrades, tense changes, relative-pronoun swaps, and dialect-to-MSA word substitutions). Use as the LEXICAL step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-lexical))
- **asr-patch-no-touch**: Extract immutable spans (proper names, legal entities, technical brands) from the COMBINE-step output of an ASR transcript. These spans become protected for the remainder of the patch chain — no replace, insert, delete, reorder, or script-swap is allowed within them. Use as the NO_TOUCH step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-no-touch))
- **asr-patch-numeral**: Format-only normalization of years, dates, and percentages to canonical digits in an ASR transcript segment. Keeps 2-digit years unless a century anchor disambiguates. Normalizes unambiguous percentages to number+%. Preserves readout: never alters pronunciation or relative position of numbers. Aligns numeral style with ENGLISH_TRANSLATION only when multiple valid forms exist and meaning is unchanged. Use as the NUMERAL step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-numeral))
- **asr-patch-preprocess**: PHASE 1 preprocessing for ASR transcript patching. Tokenizes ORIGINAL, produces a faithful English reference translation, estimates aggressiveness_level (low/medium/high), determines speaker_scope (single/multi), and predicts segment-start / segment-end sentence-boundary confidence. Always run once before any PHASE 2 patch step. Outputs tokenization, translation, aggressiveness_level, speaker_scope, seg_start, seg_end. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-preprocess))
- **asr-patch-punct**: Restore sentence-ending marks, commas after discourse markers, and clause-boundary punctuation in an ASR transcript segment. Mirrors the punctuation density of ENGLISH_TRANSLATION, scaled by aggressiveness_level. Handles run-ons by splitting into multiple sentences. Downgrades spurious full stops in over-segmented input. Uses seg_end to decide terminal punctuation. Use as the PUNCT step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-punct))
- **asr-patch-remain-fix**: Final residual cleanup of an ASR transcript segment. Repairs only misses from earlier ACTIVE chain steps already executed in this run — never backfills edits belonging to inactive steps. Preserves no_touch_tokens and legal/entity protections, preserves language/script variant, avoids adjacent redundant punctuation, and prefers no-edit when confidence is low. Aligns intensity with aggressiveness_level. Use as the final REMAIN_FIX step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-remain-fix))
- **asr-patch-router**: Orchestrator for ASR/STT transcript patching. Inspects an input transcript segment, decides which per-step patch skills are needed (preprocess, speaker, combine, no-touch, lexical, disfluency, format, numeral, punct, casing, remain-fix), and runs them in the canonical order. Use whenever the user asks to clean up, patch, normalize, or post-process an ASR transcript, or supplies fields like ORIGINAL, ENGLISH_TRANSLATION, {chain_steps}, {input_transcript}, {locale}, {prev_context}, {next_context}. Selects standard vs conservative mode and assembles the final JSON output that matches the fixed s… ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-router))
- **asr-patch-speaker**: Normalize speaker labels in an ASR transcript segment. Converts existing labels like "Speaker 1:" or "Abc:" to canonical "[SPK1]:" / "[ABC]:" form, or adds [SPK1]:, [SPK2]: tags when speaker_scope is multi but labels are missing. Pass-through when speaker_scope is single and no labels exist. Use as the SPEAKER step of the ASR patch chain. ([yushims/trans_refine](https://github.com/yushims/trans_refine/tree/HEAD/claude_skill/asr-patch-speaker))
- **asr-picker**: Pick ASR model, decoding strategy, chunking, and LM fusion for a given deployment target. Use when you need help with asr picker. ([anubhavg-icpl/vibe](https://github.com/anubhavg-icpl/vibe/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-picker) · ★13)
- **asr-pipeline**: ASR 知识管道主入口。接收 Raw 笔记或粘贴转写，先登记 51 Raw，再调用 asr-cleaner 只清洗不归档，询问方向后分流到自我面试、HR 旁观面试、会议分享（台上演讲/黑客松），或通用归档，并在 52 Wiki 生成文档包。用户说「处理这段 ASR」「面试 ASR」「黑客松转写」「台上分享做成 Wiki」时使用。不接收音频视频；一面二面对照用 interview-round-compare。 ([CamellionKid/asr-wiki-pipeline](https://github.com/CamellionKid/asr-wiki-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-pipeline))
- **asr-polish**: 将半小时以上视频、课程、访谈或播客的中文、英文及中英混合 ASR 转写整理为保真、流畅的完整阅读稿；修正有依据的误识别、清理口语噪音、统一术语并处理长文本衔接。仅在用户明确声明全英文并要求译稿时，另附格式对应的中文译稿。 ([Starxy/skills](https://github.com/Starxy/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-polish))
- **asr-python-api**: Use WhisperX's Python API for ASR model loading, audio arrays, VAD-batched transcription, progress callbacks, local cache use, and device/memory tuning. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/whisperx/sub-skills/asr-python-api) · ★357 · 2 copies)
- **asr-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to an American Sociological Review (ASR) revise-and-resubmit. ASR R&Rs typically demand substantial revision and reviewers may span different methodological traditions, so the response must convert each reviewer while keeping the editor confident. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/American-Sociological-Review-Skills/skills/asr-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 4 copies)
- **asr-recursive-improvement-loop**: Run one cycle of a recursive improvement loop for a personal or fine-tuned speech-to-text model. Reads the transcript history since the last review, flags clear mistranscriptions, confirms with the user what they meant, adds confirmed words to an error list and prepares targeted sentences for the next incremental fine-tune. Use for "review my transcripts", "what has my ASR model been getting wrong", "mine my dictation history", "run the improvement loop", or setting this loop up for an ASR project. ([danielrosehill/ASR-Recursive-Improvement-Loop](https://github.com/danielrosehill/ASR-Recursive-Improvement-Loop/tree/HEAD/skills/asr-recursive-improvement-loop) · ★1)

- **assembly-metrics-comparison-across-assemblers**: Use when multiple FASTQ metagenomic samples (e.g., HMP or Horizon oil-spill Illumina reads) are being assembled and you need to decide whether a merged contig set outperforms individual assembler/kmer runs on contig count, contiguity, and assembled bases. - **assembly-metrics-reporting**: Use when you need to quantify inconsistent-overlap reduction between PECAT's first-round (haplotype-collapsed) and second-round (SNP-phased) string graphs on diploid long-read datasets, e.g. to validate that phasing-based overlap filtering worked. - **assembly-quality-assessment**: Use when preprocessed paired-end metagenomic reads must be de novo assembled and the resulting contigs must be quality-checked via contig count, total length (bp), and N50, e.g. to reproduce VIRGO's assembly cohort statistics. - **assembly-quality-assessment-metrics**: Use when assemblies from multiple de novo assemblers must be scored per contig against known reference replicons, requiring a Phred-like quality metric derived from alignment identity rather than raw sequence features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-assessment-metrics))
- **assembly-quality-assessment-quast**: Use when you have long-read assemblies (raw Miniasm/Flye assemblies, CONSENT-polished, or RACON-polished) plus a reference genome and need standardized metrics (NGA50, NGA75, misassemblies, genome fraction, errors per 100 kbp) to compare correction or polishing tools. - **assembly-quality-assessment-with-quast**: Use when two shotgun read sets (e.g. a chemically treated library such as RIMS-seq and its untreated control) have each been assembled de novo and you must quantify whether treatment altered assembly quality via N50 and mismatch rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/assembly-quality-assessment-with-quast))
- **assembly-quality-evaluation-with-quast**: Use when FASTQ read sets (simulated or experimental nanopore) have been assembled de novo and consensus quality must be scored per genome against a ground-truth reference to judge simulator or sequencer fidelity. - **assembly-quality-index-calculation**: Use when a draft genome assembly needs a numeric regional/structural quality score (R-AQI/S-AQI) based on clipped-read error counts, or when heterozygous loci must be separated from true assembly errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-index-calculation))
- **assembly-quality-metric-computation**: Use when assemblies have been produced at multiple long-read coverage levels (e.g., 5x-45x PacBio) and you need per-level NGA50 and genome fraction values, aligned to a reference genome, to identify coverage plateaus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-metric-computation))
- **assembly-quality-metric-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have a per-assembly metrics table (AQI scores plus LAI, QV, BUSCO, N50) for multiple assemblies and need to test whether CRAQ's R-AQI/S-AQI measure the same quality dimensions as existing metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-metric-correlation-analysis))
- **assembly-quality-overcorrection-assessment**: Use when a polished HiFi/T2T assembly must be validated against its draft: you need to confirm QV gains, reduction of homozygous high-quality variants, and absence of overcorrection or switch errors before accepting the polished consensus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-overcorrection-assessment))
- **assembly-quality-score-geometric-mean-aggregation**: Use when scoring assembled Illumina paired-end (FASTQ, PHRED/CASAVA qualities) sequences by overlap base agreement and filtering them against a quality threshold such as p = 0.9 or p = 0.6. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-score-geometric-mean-aggregation))
- **assembly-quality-score-thresholding**: Use when PANDAseq has assembled Illumina overlapping paired-end (e.g. 16S rRNA amplicon) reads and you must choose how many low-confidence assemblies to retain or discard via the assembly quality score cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-score-thresholding))
- **assembly-quality-threshold-filtering**: Use when paired-end Illumina FASTQ reads have been assembled (or must be filtered) and you need to set an accept/discard cutoff on the PANDAseq assembly quality score to control sequence recovery, as for the 673 845-read M. capsulatus 16S rRNA library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-threshold-filtering))
- **assembly-read-mapping-filtering**: Use when assembling untargeted nanopore FASTQ reads from samples with mixed viral abundances (e.g., co-infections), where an initial Canu assembly only recovers the most abundant genomes and residual reads must be re-assembled iteratively. - **assembly-reference**: Use when reviewing assembly for known anti-patterns, weighing contested questions (hand-written asm vs compilers, CISC vs RISC, fixed-width SIMD vs scalable vectors, AVX-512's design, whether and which ISA to learn first), checking whether an ISA or microarchitecture claim is still current (snapshot verified August 2026), finding the authoritative vendor manuals, performance references, books, and people, or needing the quick-reference numbers, first moves, and hand-written-assembly review checklist.
